# Bibliography

*Merged and deduplicated from the References sections of the 23 verified notes files in `research/notes/` (1134 unique works). Each entry lists the notes files that discuss it; open those files for the method notes.*

## Notes files

- **[01-instruction-evolution](../research/notes/01-instruction-evolution.md)** — Instruction evolution & complexification for SFT data (Evol-Instruct family and successors)
- **[02-math-problem-synthesis](../research/notes/02-math-problem-synthesis.md)** — Math problem synthesis and difficulty escalation (informal math)
- **[03-formal-math-geometry-inequalities](../research/notes/03-formal-math-geometry-inequalities.md)** — Formal theorem proving, geometry and inequality synthesis (symbolic/verified generation of hard problems)
- **[04-code-and-swe-tasks](../research/notes/04-code-and-swe-tasks.md)** — Code and software-engineering task synthesis & complexification
- **[05-procedural-puzzles-environments](../research/notes/05-procedural-puzzles-environments.md)** — Procedural reasoning environments & logic puzzles with difficulty knobs (RLVR gyms)
- **[06-agentic-tool-use-web-tasks](../research/notes/06-agentic-tool-use-web-tasks.md)** — Agentic, tool-use and deep-research/web task synthesis (compositional and obfuscation-based)
- **[07-multihop-and-long-context](../research/notes/07-multihop-and-long-context.md)** — Multi-hop composition, knowledge-grounded question composition, and long-context task synthesis
- **[08-instruction-following-constraints](../research/notes/08-instruction-following-constraints.md)** — Composable verifiable constraints for instruction following (SFT and RLVR)
- **[09-self-play-and-curriculum-rl](../research/notes/09-self-play-and-curriculum-rl.md)** — Self-play, proposer-solver co-evolution, and automatic curricula for LLM RL
- **[10-theory-and-empirics-of-difficulty](../research/notes/10-theory-and-empirics-of-difficulty.md)** — Theory & empirical science: easy-to-hard generalization, skill composition, and why difficulty matters for SFT/RL
- **[11-verification-and-quality-control](../research/notes/11-verification-and-quality-control.md)** — Verification, answer correctness, difficulty calibration and quality control for synthetic hard tasks
- **[12-frontier-lab-practices](../research/notes/12-frontier-lab-practices.md)** — Frontier-lab and open-model technical reports: how they source, synthesize and filter HARD prompts for SFT and RL
- **[13-multimodal-science-sql-other-domains](../research/notes/13-multimodal-science-sql-other-domains.md)** — Other domains: multimodal/visual reasoning, science, text-to-SQL/tables, and non-verifiable domains
- **[14-dynamic-eval-and-open-endedness](../research/notes/14-dynamic-eval-and-open-endedness.md)** — Dynamic benchmark generation, adversarial filtering, and open-ended/quality-diversity task generation
- **[15-gui-computer-use-task-synthesis](../research/notes/15-gui-computer-use-task-synthesis.md)** — GUI, computer-use and browser task synthesis and RL curricula (CUA)
- **[16-terminal-and-workspace-agent-tasks](../research/notes/16-terminal-and-workspace-agent-tasks.md)** — Terminal/CLI and data-analysis, spreadsheet and office agent tasks: recursive easy-to-hard escalation of execution-verified seeds
- **[17-learned-generators-simulated-envs](../research/notes/17-learned-generators-simulated-envs.md)** — Learned task generators, LLM-simulated environments and agent-environment co-evolution, including the DeepSeek-V4.1-Flash update to the frontier-lab picture
- **[18-cost-model-and-rl-infrastructure](../research/notes/18-cost-model-and-rl-infrastructure.md)** — Cost model and infrastructure: cost per accepted in-band task, generator choice, rollout allocation, RL frameworks, sandboxes and environment packaging
- **[19-dose-mixing-and-measurement-rigor](../research/notes/19-dose-mixing-and-measurement-rigor.md)** — Dose, mixing and measurement: how many hard tasks, synthetic:real blends, eval noise and decision protocols, and generator-signature audits
- **[20-learnability-and-difficulty-calibration](../research/notes/20-learnability-and-difficulty-calibration.md)** — Making hard synthetic tasks learnable: behavior priming, mid-training, meta-task transforms, corpus-derived RL tasks, and 2026 difficulty-calibration science
- **[21-interaction-structure-operators](../research/notes/21-interaction-structure-operators.md)** — Interaction-structure complexification: stacking tasks beyond the context window (memory RL), sharded and underspecified multi-turn tasks, cooperative multi-agent tasks, and adversarial injections in environments
- **[22-sound-code-verifiers-and-induction](../research/notes/22-sound-code-verifiers-and-induction.md)** — Sound and non-saturating code verifiers: formally verified code (Verus/Dafny/Lean) self-play, ARC-style program-induction generators, and performance/kernel objectives
- **[23-sourcing-licensing-and-references](../research/notes/23-sourcing-licensing-and-references.md)** — Build vs source: open hard-task datasets and environment blends, research-mined and expert tasks, licensing and provenance of generator outputs, and the survey/classic reference backbone
